Row 571 of `sources/jagy_kaplansky_schiemann/regular_ternaries.txt` gives the coefficients $(a, b, c, d, e, f) = (13, 21, 28, 12, 4, 6)$ of $Q = ax^2 + by^2 + cz^2 + dyz + ezx + fxy$; the Gram tensor is that of $b(x, x) = Q(x)$.
